\displaystyle w_{1}(\G_{p,0,r}\mu)\leqslant{}

\displaystyle w_{1}(\mu)+(1-p)(1-r)\{-r-2p^{2}-3r^{2}-2pr+3p^{2}r+6pr^{2}-3p^{2}r^{2}\}\{\mu(WD)-\mu(WWDD)

\displaystyle-\mu(DWDD)-\mu(WDL)\}-\{1-(1+p+pr-p^{2}r)(1-p)(1-r)(1+r)\}\mu(DD)-r^{2}(1-r)^{2}

\displaystyle(1-p)^{2}\mu(WLD)-r^{2}(1-r)^{2}(1-p)^{2}\mu(DLD)+\{p-r+r^{2}(1-p)\}(1-p)^{2}(1-r)\mu(WWDW)

\displaystyle+p(1-p)^{2}(1-r)^{2}\mu(WWWD)+(1-p)(1-r)(p-r-3p^{2}-3r^{2}-pr+2p^{2}r+6pr^{2})

\displaystyle\mu(WWDD)-(1-p)(1-r)\{2p-4r+p^{2}+3r^{2}+2pr-2p^{2}r-6pr^{2}\}\G_{p,0,r}\mu(LD)-(1-p)

\displaystyle(1-r)\{3r+2p^{2}+2r^{2}-pr-2p^{2}r-4pr^{2}-r^{3}\}\G_{p,0,r}\mu(LDW)-(1-p)^{2}(1-r)(1-r^{2}+pr^{2})

\displaystyle\G_{p,0,r}\mu(LDL)-(1-p)(1-r)\{2p+r+p^{2}+2r^{2}-p^{2}r-5pr^{2}\}\G_{p,0,r}\mu(LWD)

\displaystyle\leqslant{}

\displaystyle w_{1}(\mu)+(1-p)(1-r)\{-r-2p^{2}-3r^{2}-2pr+3p^{2}r+6pr^{2}-3p^{2}r^{2}\}\{\mu(WD)-\mu(WWDD)

\displaystyle-\mu(DWDD)-\mu(WDL)\}-\{1-(1+p+pr-p^{2}r)(1-p)(1-r)(1+r)\}\mu(DD)-r^{2}(1-r)^{2}

\displaystyle(1-p)^{2}\mu(WLD)-r^{2}(1-r)^{2}(1-p)^{2}\mu(DLD)+\{p-r+r^{2}(1-p)\}(1-p)^{2}(1-r)\mu(WWDW)

\displaystyle+p(1-p)^{2}(1-r)^{2}\mu(WWWD)+(1-p)(1-r)(p-r-3p^{2}-3r^{2}-pr+2p^{2}r+6pr^{2})

\displaystyle\mu(WWDD)-(1-p)^{3}(1-r)^{2}\{2p-4r+p^{2}+3r^{2}+2pr-2p^{2}r-6pr^{2}\}\mu(WWD)-p(1-p)^{3}

\displaystyle(1-r)^{2}\{3r+2p^{2}+2r^{2}-pr-2p^{2}r-4pr^{2}-r^{3}\}\{\mu(WWDW)+\mu(WWDD)\}-r(1-p)^{5}

\displaystyle(1-r)^{2}(1-r^{2}+pr^{2})\mu(WWDW)-r^{2}(1-p)^{5}(1-r)^{2}(1-r^{2}+pr^{2})\mu(WWDD)-p(1-p)^{3}

\displaystyle(1-r)^{2}\{2p+r+p^{2}+2r^{2}-p^{2}r-5pr^{2}\}\{\mu(WWDW)+\mu(WWWD)\}-p(1-p)^{3}(1-r)^{2}

(9.26)

\displaystyle(1+r)\{2p+r+p^{2}+2r^{2}-p^{2}r-5pr^{2}\}\mu(WWDD).

We make use of an inequality analogous to (
8.78
), so that

(i)

the coefficient of
\mu(WWDD)
is updated to

\displaystyle(1-p)(1-r)(p-r-3p^{2}-3r^{2}-pr+2p^{2}r+6pr^{2})-\frac{1}{2}(1-p)^{3}(1-r)^{2}\{2p-4r+p^{2}+3r^{2}+2pr-2p^{2}r

\displaystyle-6pr^{2}\}-p(1-p)^{3}(1-r)^{2}\{3r+2p^{2}+2r^{2}-pr-2p^{2}r-4pr^{2}-r^{3}\}-r^{2}(1-p)^{5}(1-r)^{2}(1-r^{2}+pr^{2})

\displaystyle-p(1-p)^{3}(1-r)^{2}(1+r)\{2p+r+p^{2}+2r^{2}-p^{2}r-5pr^{2}\}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle(1-p)(1-r)\Big[-p^{5}r^{4}(1-r)-p^{5}r^{3}-p^{5}r^{2}-p^{5}(3-5r)-p^{4}r^{3}-\frac{5p^{4}r}{2}(3-4r)-p^{3}r(4+12r-4r^{2}-r^{3}

\displaystyle-10r^{2})-10p^{2}r^{5}-\frac{p^{2}r^{2}}{2}(13-3r-6r^{2})-pr^{3}(6+4r-5r^{2})-r^{5}+\frac{7p^{4}}{2}+p^{3}+\frac{29p^{2}r}{2}-\frac{7p^{2}}{2}+20pr^{2}

\displaystyle-9pr+r^{4}+\frac{5r^{3}}{2}-\frac{15r^{2}}{2}+r\Big]

\displaystyle\leqslant{}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(1-p)(1-r)[2r-7p^{2}-15r^{2}-18pr+29p^{2}r+40pr^{2}+2p^{3}+5r^{3}+7p^{4}+2r^{4}]

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(1-p)(1-r)\Big[\left\{\frac{9p^{3}}{2}+16p^{2}r-\frac{3p^{2}}{2}+\frac{43pr^{2}}{2}-8pr+5r^{3}-\frac{11r^{2}}{2}+2r\right\}-\frac{11p^{2}}{2}-\frac{19r^{2}}{2}-10pr

\displaystyle+13p^{2}r+\frac{37pr^{2}}{2}-\frac{5p^{3}}{2}+7p^{4}+2r^{4}\Big]

(9.27)

\displaystyle\leqslant{}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(1-p)(1-r)\left\{\frac{9p^{3}}{2}+16p^{2}r-\frac{3p^{2}}{2}+\frac{43pr^{2}}{2}-8pr+5r^{3}-\frac{11r^{2}}{2}+2r\right\}

for

and

sufficiently small, and this final upper bound is non-positive because of (

);

(ii)

the coefficient of
\mu(WWDW)
is updated to

\displaystyle(1-p)^{2}(1-r)\{p-r+r^{2}(1-p)\}-\frac{1}{2}(1-p)^{3}(1-r)^{2}\{2p-4r+p^{2}+3r^{2}+2pr-2p^{2}r-6pr^{2}\}

\displaystyle-p(1-p)^{3}(1-r)^{2}\{3r+2p^{2}+2r^{2}-pr-2p^{2}r-4pr^{2}-r^{3}\}-r(1-p)^{5}(1-r)^{2}(1-r^{2}+pr^{2})

\displaystyle-p(1-p)^{3}(1-r)^{2}\{2p+r+p^{2}+2r^{2}-p^{2}r-5pr^{2}\}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle(1-p)^{2}(1-r)\Big[-p^{4}r^{4}-p^{4}r(6-3r-r^{2})-p^{3}r^{2}(11-5r-4r^{2})-\frac{p^{3}}{2}(1-5r-6p)-5p^{2}r^{4}-3p^{2}r^{3}

(9.28)

\displaystyle-\frac{p^{2}}{2}(3-11r-8r^{2})-\frac{pr^{3}}{2}(11-6r)-\frac{pr}{2}(6-11r)-\frac{r^{2}}{2}(3-5r+2r^{2})\Big]

and

sufficiently small;

the coefficient of
\mu(WWWD)
is updated to

\displaystyle p(1-p)^{2}(1-r)^{2}-\frac{1}{2}(1-p)^{3}(1-r)^{2}\{2p-4r+p^{2}+3r^{2}+2pr-2p^{2}r-6pr^{2}\}

\displaystyle-p(1-p)^{3}(1-r)^{2}\{2p+r+p^{2}+2r^{2}-p^{2}r-5pr^{2}\}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle(1-p)^{2}(1-r)^{2}\left\{2r-\frac{3p^{2}}{2}-\frac{3r^{2}}{2}-4pr+3p^{2}r+\frac{5pr^{2}}{2}+\frac{3p^{3}}{2}+4p^{2}r^{2}-5p^{3}r^{2}+p^{4}-p^{4}r\right\}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle-p^{6}r^{3}-3p^{6}r(1-r)-\frac{p^{5}}{2}(1-2p-6r)-5p^{5}r^{4}-\frac{p^{5}r^{2}}{2}(19-24r)

\displaystyle-p^{4}r^{3}(26-14r)-\frac{p^{4}}{2}(7-18r-13r^{2})-\frac{21p^{3}r^{4}}{2}-p^{3}r(19-14r-11r^{2})+\frac{9p^{3}}{2}-\frac{5p^{2}r^{4}}{2}

\displaystyle-p^{2}r^{2}(30-18r)+16p^{2}r-\frac{3p^{2}}{2}-\frac{pr^{3}}{2}(38-11r)+\frac{43pr^{2}}{2}-8pr-\frac{3r^{4}}{2}+5r^{3}-\frac{11r^{2}}{2}+2r

(9.29)

\displaystyle\leqslant{}

\displaystyle\frac{9p^{3}}{2}+16p^{2}r-\frac{3p^{2}}{2}+\frac{43pr^{2}}{2}-8pr+5r^{3}-\frac{11r^{2}}{2}+2r

which is non-positive precisely because of the inequality in (
9.13
).

A final step that we perform now is the deduction of a suitable upper bound for the coefficient of
\mu(DD)
in (
9.26
) (using (

) with

):

\displaystyle-\{1-(1+p+pr-p^{2}r)(1-p)(1-r)(1+r)\}=-(1-p)(1-r)\{(1-p)(1-r)\}^{-1}

\displaystyle+(1+p+pr-p^{2}r)(1-p)(1-r)(1+r)

\displaystyle\leqslant{}

\displaystyle-(1-p)(1-r)(1+p+r+p^{2}+r^{2}+pr-2p^{2}r-2pr^{2}+p^{2}r^{2})

\displaystyle+(1-p)(1-r)(1+p+r+2pr-p^{2}r+pr^{2}-p^{2}r^{2})

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle-(1-p)(1-r)(p^{2}+r^{2}-pr-p^{2}r-3pr^{2}+2p^{2}r^{2})

(9.30)

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle-(1-p)(1-r)\{p^{2}+r^{2}-2pr+pr(1-p-3r+2pr)\}\leqslant-(1-p)(1-r)(p-r)^{2},

where the last inequality is true for

and

sufficiently small. Incorporating (
9.27
), (
9.28
), (
9.29
) and (

) into (

), we get the updated weight function inequality

\displaystyle w_{1}(\G_{p,0,r}\mu)\leqslant{}

\displaystyle w_{1}(\mu)+(1-p)(1-r)\{-r-2p^{2}-3r^{2}-2pr+3p^{2}r+6pr^{2}-3p^{2}r^{2}\}\{\mu(WD)

\displaystyle-\mu(WWDD)-\mu(DWDD)-\mu(WDL)\}-(1-p)(1-r)(p-r)^{2}\mu(DD)

\displaystyle-r^{2}(1-r)^{2}(1-p)^{2}\mu(WLD)-r^{2}(1-r)^{2}(1-p)^{2}\mu(DLD)

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{9p^{3}}{2}+16p^{2}r-\frac{3p^{2}}{2}+\frac{43pr^{2}}{2}-8pr+5r^{3}-\frac{11r^{2}}{2}+2r\right)\mu(WWWD)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}(1-p)(1-r)\left(\frac{9p^{3}}{2}+16p^{2}r-\frac{3p^{2}}{2}+\frac{43pr^{2}}{2}-8pr+5r^{3}-\frac{11r^{2}}{2}+2r\right)\mu(WWDD)

\displaystyle(1-p)^{2}(1-r)\Big[-p^{4}r^{4}-p^{4}r(6-3r-r^{2})-p^{3}r^{2}(11-5r-4r^{2})-\frac{p^{3}}{2}(1-5r-6p)

\displaystyle-5p^{2}r^{4}-3p^{2}r^{3}-\frac{p^{2}}{2}(3-11r-8r^{2})-\frac{pr^{3}}{2}(11-6r)-\frac{pr}{2}(6-11r)-\frac{r^{2}}{2}(3-5r+2r^{2})\Big]

\displaystyle\mu(WWDW).

At this point, we remove all terms, except the one involving
\mu(DD)
, from the right side of the inequality above, since the coefficient of each of these terms is non-positive as long as

and

are sufficiently small and (
9.13
) holds. The final weight function inequality obtained is exactly what we have in (

) once we set

,

and

to be as in (

), and we replace

by

. This concludes the construction of our weight function for the envelope PCA
\E_{r^{\prime},s^{\prime}}
(correspondingly, for
\G_{p,q,r}
with

,

and

are as given by (
9.12
)), when
(r^{\prime},s^{\prime})
belong to the regime in
(B1)
.

9.3.
Detailed construction of the weight function for the bond percolation game when
(r^{\prime},s^{\prime})
belongs to the regime given by
(B2)

In the regime described by
(B2)
, we set
s^{\prime}=0
and
r^{\prime}>0
. We begin again, as in §
8.3
and §
9.2
, with
c_{1}=1
and
\mathcal{C}_{1}=(D)_{0}
, followed by
c_{2}=1
and
\mathcal{C}_{2}=(W,D)_{0,1}
. We skip some of the details in the computation of the pushforward measures (i.e.
\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu(D)
,
\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu(WD)
etc.) as these are special cases (obtained by setting
s^{\prime}=0
) of what we have already computed in §
9.2
. In each computation that follows, we indicate by underbraces, wherever necessary, which terms are being combined together to proceed to the next step:

(9.31)

\displaystyle\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu(D)={}

\displaystyle 2(1-r^{\prime})\mu(WD)+(1+r^{\prime})(1-r^{\prime})\mu(DD)+2r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})\mu(LD),

\displaystyle\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu(WD)={}

\displaystyle\underbrace{(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LDW)+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LDL)+(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LDD)}_{\text{combine using }\mu(LD)=\mu(LDW)+\mu(LDL)+\mu(LDD)}+(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LWD)

(9.32)

\displaystyle+\underbrace{r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(WLD)+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LLD)+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(DLD)}_{\text{combine using }\mu(LD)=\mu(WLD)+\mu(LLD)+\mu(DLD)}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\underbrace{(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LD)-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LDW)-(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LDL)}_{\text{obtained from the first combination}}+(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LWD)

\displaystyle+\underbrace{r^{\prime}(1+r^{\prime})(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LD)-r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(WLD)-r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(DLD)}_{\text{obtained from the second combination}}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle(1+r^{\prime})^{2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LD)+(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LWD)-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LDW)-(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LDL)

(9.33)

\displaystyle-r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(WLD)-r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(DLD),

and we can write
\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu(LWD)=C_{1}-C_{2}
, where

\displaystyle C_{1}={}

\displaystyle r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LDW)+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LDD)+r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LDL)+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LWD)

(9.34)

\displaystyle+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(WLD)+r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LLD)+r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(DLD)

and

\displaystyle C_{2}={}

\displaystyle r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(DLDW)+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(LLDW)+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(DLDD)+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LLDD)

\displaystyle+r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(DLDL)+r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(LLDL)+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(LLWD)+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(DLWD)

\displaystyle+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(LWLD)+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(DWLD)+r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LLLD)+r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(DLLD)

(9.35)

\displaystyle+r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(LDLD)+r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(DDLD).

It is worthwhile to note here that
each
term of
each
of
C_{1}
and
C_{2}
is non-negative. Another crucial observation is that, in
\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu(LWD)=C_{1}-C_{2}
, the coefficient corresponding to
each
term is of the order of
r^{\prime}
(or smaller), so that when
r^{\prime}
is small, each term ought to be negligible. We now set our initial choice of the weight function to be (just as in (
8.17
) of §
8.3
, as well as our initial choice in §
9.2
)
w_{0}(\mu)=\mu(D)+\mu(WD)+\mu(LWD)
. This yields the weight function inequality (which, in this case, is an identity):

\displaystyle w_{0}\left(\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu\right)={}

\displaystyle\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu(D)+\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu(WD)+\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu(LWD)

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle 2(1-r^{\prime})\mu(WD)+(1+r^{\prime})(1-r^{\prime})\mu(DD)+2r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})\mu(LD)+(1+r^{\prime})^{2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LD)

\displaystyle+(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LWD)-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LDW)-(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LDL)-r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(WLD)

\displaystyle-r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(DLD)+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LDW)+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LDD)+r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}

\displaystyle\mu(LDL)+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LWD)+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(WLD)+r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LLD)

\displaystyle+r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(DLD)-C_{2}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\underbrace{2\mu(WD)}-2r^{\prime}\mu(WD)+\underbrace{\mu(DD)}-r^{\prime 2}\mu(DD)+\underbrace{\mu(LD)}+(2r^{\prime}-4r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 4})\mu(LD)

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\mu(LWD)}-(r^{\prime}+r^{\prime 2}-r^{\prime 3})\mu(LWD)-(1-r^{\prime})^{3}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LDL)+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(WLD)

\displaystyle+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LDD)+r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LLD)-C_{2}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\underbrace{w_{0}(\mu)}_{\text{combining underbraced terms above}}-2r^{\prime}\mu(WD)-r^{\prime 2}\mu(DD)+\underbrace{(2r^{\prime}-4r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 4})\mu(LD)}

\displaystyle-(r^{\prime}+r^{\prime 2}-r^{\prime 3})\mu(LWD)-(1-r^{\prime})^{3}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LDL)+\underbrace{r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(WLD)}

(9.36)

\displaystyle+\underbrace{r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LDD)}+\underbrace{r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LLD)}-C_{2}.

While of the same form as (
8.2
), the inequality in (
9.36
) does not satisfy (
8.3
): in the underbraced terms in the final expression of (
9.36
), the coefficients
r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}
,
r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})
and
r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})
are non-negative (in fact, strictly positive for
r^{\prime}\in(0,1)
), and the coefficient
(2r^{\prime}-4r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 4})
is also strictly positive for
r^{\prime}\in(0,0.53918)
. Therefore, further adjustments are necessary to our initial weight function.

Note, here, that
-2r^{\prime}\mu(WD)
is one of the non-positive terms in the right side of (
9.36
), and it is worthwhile to check whether
-2r^{\prime}\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu(WD)
can be used to negate some of the positive terms (indicated by underbraces) in the final expression of (
9.36
). From (
9.32
), we see that
-2r^{\prime}\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu(WD)
contributes the terms

-2r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LDW)-2r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LDL)-2r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LDD),

as well as the terms

\displaystyle-2r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(WLD)-2r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LLD)-2r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(DLD)

(9.37)

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle-2r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LD)-2r^{\prime 3}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LLD),

and these can be used to negate, partially, the term
(2r^{\prime}-4r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 4})\mu(LD)
on the right side of (
9.36
) when the coefficient
(2r^{\prime}-4r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 4})
is positive. This serves as a good motivation for us to set the first adjustment to be
w_{1}(\mu)=w_{0}(\mu)-2r^{\prime}\mu(WD)
. This adjustment updates the weight function inequality of (
9.36
) to:

\displaystyle w_{1}\left(\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu\right)={}

\displaystyle w_{1}(\mu)+2r^{\prime}\mu(WD)-2r^{\prime}\mu(WD)-r^{\prime 2}\mu(DD)+(2r^{\prime}-4r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 4})\mu(LD)-(r^{\prime}+r^{\prime 2}-r^{\prime 3})\mu(LWD)

\displaystyle-(1-r^{\prime})^{3}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LDL)+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(WLD)+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LDD)

\displaystyle+r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LLD)-C_{2}-2r(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LDW)-2r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LDL)

\displaystyle-2r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LDD)-2r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LWD)\underbrace{-2r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(WLD)}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-2r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LLD)-2r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(DLD)}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle w_{1}(\mu)-r^{\prime 2}\mu(DD)\underbrace{+(2r^{\prime}-4r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 4})\mu(LD)}\underbrace{-(r^{\prime}+r^{\prime 2}-r^{\prime 3})\mu(LWD)}_{\text{(i)}}\underbrace{-(1-r^{\prime})^{3}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LDL)}_{\text{(ii)}}

\displaystyle+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(WLD)\underbrace{+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LDD)}_{\text{(iii)}}\underbrace{+r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LLD)}-C_{2}

\displaystyle-2r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LDW)\underbrace{-2r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LDL)}_{\text{(ii)}}\underbrace{-2r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LDD)}_{\text{(iii)}}\underbrace{-2r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LWD)}_{\text{(i)}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-2r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LD)-2r^{\prime 3}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LLD)}_{\text{combining the underbraced terms in the previous step using \eqref{combination_1}}}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle w_{1}(\mu)-r^{\prime 2}\mu(DD)\underbrace{+(2r^{\prime}-6r^{\prime 2}+4r^{\prime 3}-r^{\prime 4})\mu(LD)}_{\text{combining underbraced terms involving }\mu(LD)}\underbrace{-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})\mu(LWD)}_{\text{combining underbraced terms labeled (i)}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime 2})\mu(LDL)}_{\text{combining underbraced terms labeled (ii)}}+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(WLD)\underbrace{-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LDD)}_{\text{combining underbraced terms labeled (iii)}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{+r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(LLD)}_{\text{combining underbraced terms involving }\mu(LLD)}-2r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LDW)-C_{2}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle w_{1}(\mu)-r^{\prime 2}\mu(DD)+(2r^{\prime}-6r^{\prime 2}+4r^{\prime 3}-r^{\prime 4})\mu(LD)-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})\mu(LWD)

\displaystyle\underbrace{-(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime 2})\mu(LDL)-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LDD)-2r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LDW)}_{\text{obtain }-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(LD)\text{ from these}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(WLD)+r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(LLD)}_{\text{obtain }r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(LD)\text{ from these}}-C_{2}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle w_{1}(\mu)-r^{\prime 2}\mu(DD)+(2r^{\prime}-6r^{\prime 2}+4r^{\prime 3}-r^{\prime 4})\mu(LD)-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})\mu(LWD)

\displaystyle\underbrace{-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(LD)}-(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1-r^{\prime}+2r^{\prime 2})\mu(LDL)-2r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LDD)

\displaystyle-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LDW)\underbrace{+r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(LD)}-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{4}\mu(LLD)

\displaystyle-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(DLD)-C_{2}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle w_{1}(\mu)-r^{\prime 2}\mu(DD)\underbrace{+(2r^{\prime}-6r^{\prime 2}+4r^{\prime 3}-r^{\prime 4})\mu(LD)}-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})\mu(LWD)

\displaystyle-(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1-r^{\prime}+2r^{\prime 2})\mu(LDL)-2r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LDD)-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LDW)

(9.38)

\displaystyle-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{4}\mu(LLD)-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(DLD)-C_{2}.

One can see that (
9.38
) becomes the inequality in (
9.4
) if
C_{2}
, which consists of non-negative summands (as remarked right after (
9.35
)), is removed. The only problematic term on the right side of (
9.38
), which
may
prevent it from satisfying (
8.3
), has been indicated by an underbrace: the coefficient
(2r^{\prime}-6r^{\prime 2}+4r^{\prime 3}-r^{\prime 4})
of
\mu(LD)
is strictly negative for
r^{\prime}\in[0.4564,1]
, so that if we were to consider the regime where
s^{\prime}=0
and
r^{\prime}\in[0.4564,1]
, no further adjustments to our weight function would be required. Since, however, we
do
consider
r^{\prime}<0.4564
in the regime described by
(B2)
, we choose to make use of the term
-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})\mu(LWD)
, on the right side of (
9.38
), to negate,
partially
, the problematic, underbraced term
(2r^{\prime}-6r^{\prime 2}+4r^{\prime 3}-r^{\prime 4})\mu(LD)
, following the idea outlined in §
8.1.2
.

We define our second adjustment to the weight function by setting
w_{2}(\mu)=w_{1}(\mu)-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})\mu(LWD)
, which is exactly the same as (
9.5
). Recall that
\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu(LWD)=C_{1}-C_{2}
, with
C_{1}
and
C_{2}
as defined in (
9.34
) and (
9.35
). As explained in (
8.6
), incorporating the new adjustment into (
9.38
) yields

\displaystyle w_{2}\left(\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu\right)={}

\displaystyle w_{2}(\mu)\underbrace{+(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})\mu(LWD)}-r^{\prime 2}\mu(DD)+(2r^{\prime}-6r^{\prime 2}+4r^{\prime 3}-r^{\prime 4})\mu(LD)

\displaystyle\underbrace{-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})\mu(LWD)}-(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1-r^{\prime}+2r^{\prime 2})\mu(LDL)-2r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LDD)

\displaystyle-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LDW)-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{4}\mu(LLD)-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(DLD)\underbrace{-C_{2}}_{\text{(i)}}

\displaystyle-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})C_{1}\underbrace{+(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})C_{2}}_{\text{(i)}}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle w_{2}(\mu)-r^{\prime 2}\mu(DD)+(2r^{\prime}-6r^{\prime 2}+4r^{\prime 3}-r^{\prime 4})\mu(LD)\underbrace{-(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1-r^{\prime}+2r^{\prime 2})\mu(LDL)}_{\text{(i)}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-2r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LDD)}_{\text{(ii)}}\underbrace{-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LDW)}_{\text{(iii)}}\underbrace{-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{4}\mu(LLD)}_{\text{(iv)}}\underbrace{-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(DLD)}_{\text{(v)}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LDW)}_{\text{(iii)}}\underbrace{-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LDD)}_{\text{(ii)}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LDL)}_{\text{(i)}}-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LWD)

\displaystyle-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(WLD)\underbrace{-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LLD)}_{\text{(iv)}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(DLD)}_{\text{(v)}}\underbrace{-(1-r^{\prime})^{3}C_{2}}_{\text{combining underbraced terms labeled (i)}}\ \text{(using \eqref{C_{1}})}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle w_{2}(\mu)-r^{\prime 2}\mu(DD)+(2r^{\prime}-6r^{\prime 2}+4r^{\prime 3}-r^{\prime 4})\mu(LD)

\displaystyle\underbrace{-(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 5}-3r^{\prime 4}+3r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 2}-r^{\prime}+1)\mu(LDL)}_{\text{combining underbraced terms labeled (i)}}\underbrace{-r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 2}+5)\mu(LDD)}_{\text{combining underbraced terms labeled (ii)}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 3}-3r^{\prime 2}+4r^{\prime}+1)\mu(LDW)}_{\text{combining underbraced terms labeled (iii)}}\underbrace{-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 5}-2r^{\prime 4}+4r^{\prime 2}-2r^{\prime}+1)\mu(LLD)}_{\text{combining underbraced terms labeled (iv)}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 4}-3r^{\prime 3}+3r^{\prime 2}-r^{\prime}+1)\mu(DLD)}_{\text{combining underbraced terms labeled (v)}}-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LWD)

\displaystyle-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(WLD)-(1-r^{\prime})^{3}C_{2}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle w_{2}(\mu)-r^{\prime 2}\mu(DD)+(2r^{\prime}-6r^{\prime 2}+4r^{\prime 3}-r^{\prime 4})\mu(LD)

\displaystyle\underbrace{-(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 5}-3r^{\prime 4}+3r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 2}-r^{\prime}+1)\mu(LDL)-r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 2}+5)\mu(LDD)}_{\text{(vi)}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 3}-3r^{\prime 2}+4r^{\prime}+1)\mu(LDW)}_{\text{(vi)}}\underbrace{-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 5}-2r^{\prime 4}+4r^{\prime 2}-2r^{\prime}+1)\mu(LLD)}_{\text{(vii)}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 4}-3r^{\prime 3}+3r^{\prime 2}-r^{\prime}+1)\mu(DLD)-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(WLD)}_{\text{(vii)}}

(9.39)

\displaystyle-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LWD)-(1-r^{\prime})^{3}C_{2}.

Combining the terms that are grouped together using underbraces labeled (vi) in the final expression for (
9.39
), we obtain:

\displaystyle-(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 5}-3r^{\prime 4}+3r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 2}-r^{\prime}+1)\mu(LDL)-r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 2}+5)\mu(LDD)

\displaystyle-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 3}-3r^{\prime 2}+4r^{\prime}+1)\mu(LDW)

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle-r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 2}+5)\mu(LD)

\displaystyle-\left\{(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 5}-3r^{\prime 4}+3r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 2}-r^{\prime}+1)-r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 2}+5)\right\}\mu(LDL)

\displaystyle-\left\{r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 3}-3r^{\prime 2}+4r^{\prime}+1)-r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 2}+5)\right\}\mu(LDW)

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle-r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 2}+5)\mu(LD)-(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1-r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+3r^{\prime 3}-r^{\prime 4})\mu(LDL)

(9.40)

\displaystyle-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1-r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+3r^{\prime 3}-r^{\prime 4})\mu(LDW).

Combining the terms that are grouped together using underbraces labeled (vii) in the final expression for (
9.39
), we obtain:

\displaystyle-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 5}-2r^{\prime 4}+4r^{\prime 2}-2r^{\prime}+1)\mu(LLD)-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 4}-3r^{\prime 3}+3r^{\prime 2}-r^{\prime}+1)\mu(DLD)

\displaystyle-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(WLD)

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LD)

\displaystyle-\left\{r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 5}-2r^{\prime 4}+4r^{\prime 2}-2r^{\prime}+1)-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\right\}\mu(LLD)

\displaystyle-\left\{r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 4}-3r^{\prime 3}+3r^{\prime 2}-r^{\prime}+1)-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\right\}\mu(DLD)

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LD)-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 5}-2r^{\prime 4}-r^{\prime 3}+7r^{\prime 2}-5r^{\prime}+1)\mu(LLD)

(9.41)

\displaystyle-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{6}\mu(DLD).

Incorporating (
9.40
) and (
9.41
) into (
9.39
) yields:

\displaystyle w_{2}\left(\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu\right)={}

\displaystyle w_{2}(\mu)-r^{\prime 2}\mu(DD)\underbrace{+(2r^{\prime}-6r^{\prime 2}+4r^{\prime 3}-r^{\prime 4})\mu(LD)-r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 2}+5)\mu(LD)}

\displaystyle-(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1-r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+3r^{\prime 3}-r^{\prime 4})\mu(LDL)-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1-r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+3r^{\prime 3}-r^{\prime 4})\mu(LDW)

\displaystyle\underbrace{-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LD)}-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 5}-2r^{\prime 4}-r^{\prime 3}+7r^{\prime 2}-5r^{\prime}+1)\mu(LLD)

\displaystyle-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{6}\mu(DLD)-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LWD)-(1-r^{\prime})^{3}C_{2}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle w_{2}(\mu)-r^{\prime 2}\mu(DD)\underbrace{+(2r^{\prime}-14r^{\prime 2}+23r^{\prime 3}-14r^{\prime 4}+3r^{\prime 6}-r^{\prime 7})\mu(LD)}_{\text{combining the underbraced terms above}}

\displaystyle-(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1-r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+3r^{\prime 3}-r^{\prime 4})\mu(LDL)-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1-r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+3r^{\prime 3}-r^{\prime 4})\mu(LDW)

\displaystyle-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(r^{\prime 5}-2r^{\prime 4}-r^{\prime 3}+7r^{\prime 2}-5r^{\prime}+1)\mu(LLD)-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{6}\mu(DLD)

(9.42)

\displaystyle-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LWD)-(1-r^{\prime})^{3}C_{2}.

Observe that if we were to remove the term
-(1-r^{\prime})^{3}C_{2}
, keeping in mind that
C_{2}
consists of non-negative summands (as mentioned after (
9.35
)), then (
9.42
) yields the inequality in (
9.5
). Next, we note that

(i)

the polynomial
2r^{\prime}-14r^{\prime 2}+23r^{\prime 3}-14r^{\prime 4}+3r^{\prime 6}-r^{\prime 7}
is strictly negative for
r^{\prime}\in(0.201383,1]
,

(ii)

the polynomial
1-r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+3r^{\prime 3}-r^{\prime 4}
is strictly positive for all
r^{\prime}\in[0,0.52779)
,

(iii)

the polynomial
r^{\prime 5}-2r^{\prime 4}-r^{\prime 3}+7r^{\prime 2}-5r^{\prime}+1
is strictly positive for all
r^{\prime}\geqslant 0
,

so that (
9.42
) satisfies the criterion in (
8.3
) as long as we have
r^{\prime}\in(0.201383,0.52779)
. However, we wish to go beyond this range of values of
r^{\prime}
, as indicated by
(B2)
.

When
r^{\prime}\leqslant 0.201382
, the only term we need be concerned about is the one indicated by an underbrace in the final expression of (
9.42
), i.e.
(2r^{\prime}-14r^{\prime 2}+23r^{\prime 3}-14r^{\prime 4}+3r^{\prime 6}-r^{\prime 7})\mu(LD)
. We now motivate our approach for partially negating this term, though, as emphasized earlier to the reader, there is, truly, no
unique
way of proceeding. It is quite plausible that other,
similar
approaches yield somewhat better bounds, but intuitively, we do not see how our bounds can be
significantly
improved. It is the order of magnitude of the coefficient of
\mu(LD)
in (
9.42
), i.e.
2r^{\prime}
(when
r^{\prime}
is small), that prevents us from covering a regime larger than that in (
(B2)
), since the existing negative terms on the right side of (
9.42
) are collectively unable to negate this coefficient when
r^{\prime}
is “too small”. We explain this in the following paragraph.

On the right side of (
9.42
), we have, on one hand, negative terms involving
\mu(LDW)
and
\mu(LDL)
, and on the other, negative terms involving
\mu(DLD)
and
\mu(LLD)
. At a glance, what seem to be missing are negative terms involving
\mu(LDD)
and
\mu(WLD)
(had there been a couple of negative terms involving
\mu(LDD)
and
\mu(WLD)
, with coefficients whose order of magnitude is
r^{\prime}
, for
r^{\prime}
is small, on the right side of (
9.42
), these could have helped take care of
(2r^{\prime}-14r^{\prime 2}+23r^{\prime 3}-14r^{\prime 4}+3r^{\prime 6}-r^{\prime 7})\mu(LD)
, since
\mu(LDW)+\mu(LDL)+\mu(LDD)=\mu(DLD)+\mu(LLD)+\mu(WLD)=\mu(LD)
). Next, among the terms of
C_{2}
in (
9.35
), we have

(i)

the terms
2r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(DWLD)
and
r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}\mu(LWLD)
, but no term involving
\mu(WWLD)
, which is why we cannot directly obtain from
-(1-r^{\prime})^{3}C_{2}
a negative term involving
\mu(WLD)
;

(ii)

the terms
r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(DLDD)
and
r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{3}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(LLDD)
, but no term involving
\mu(WLDD)
, which is why we cannot directly obtain from
-(1-r^{\prime})^{3}C_{2}
a negative term involving
\mu(LDD)
.

We shall, therefore, avail the aid of
-r^{\prime 2}(3-3r^{\prime}+r^{\prime 2})(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LWD)
and
-r^{\prime 2}\mu(DD)
that are present in the right side of (
9.42
), and the terms
-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{6}\mu(LLWD)
and
-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{6}\mu(LLDW)
from the expansion of
-(1-r^{\prime})^{3}C_{2}
, to define the third adjustment to our weight function. It can be checked that the remaining terms of
C_{2}
, namely those involving
\mu(DLDW)
,
\mu(DLDL)
,
\mu(DDLD)
,
\mu(DDLD)
,
\mu(LLDL)
,
\mu(LDLD)
and
\mu(LLLD)
, are either of no use in generating (via the probabilities of the corresponding cylinder sets induced by the pushforward measure
\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu
) the terms that are missing above, or generate the missing terms with coefficients that are of an order of magnitude far too small to be of any significant use to us.

We set the new, adjusted weight function to be

(9.43)

\displaystyle w_{3}(\mu)={}

\displaystyle w_{2}(\mu)-r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{6}\{\mu(LLWD)+\mu(LLDW)\}-r^{\prime 2}\mu(WDD)-(3r^{\prime}-3r^{\prime 2}+r^{\prime 3})r^{\prime}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}\mu(LWD).

To understand how (
9.43
) impacts the weight function inequality in (
9.42
), we need to compute,
partially
, the pushforward measures
\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu(LLWD)
,
\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu(LLDW)
and
\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu(WDD)
. Recall that
\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu(LLWD)
equals the probability of the event
\{\E_{r^{\prime},0}\eta(0)=\E_{r^{\prime},0}\eta(1)=L,\E_{r^{\prime},0}\eta(2)=W,\E_{r^{\prime},0}\eta(3)=D\}
when
\eta
is a random configuration with law
\mu
, and we consider the contributions to
\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu(LLWD)
arising from the following events
only
:

(i)

the contribution of the event
\{\eta(0)=\eta(1)=W,\eta(2)=L,\eta(3)=D\}
to
\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu(LLWD)
is

\displaystyle w_{3}(\mu)\underbrace{+r^{\prime}(2-16r^{\prime}+21r^{\prime 2}+4r^{\prime 3}-39r^{\prime 4}+50r^{\prime 5}-35r^{\prime 6}+7r^{\prime 7}+13r^{\prime 8}-14r^{\prime 9}+6r^{\prime 10}-r^{\prime 11})\mu(LD)}

\displaystyle(\text{once again, the underbraced terms in the step above are obtained by combining the}

.

Among the problematic terms, indicated by underbraces, on the right side of the final expression of (

We partially compute
\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu(LWLD)
, which is the probability of the event
\{\E_{r^{\prime},0}\eta(0)=L,\E_{r^{\prime},0}\eta(1)=W,\E_{r^{\prime},0}\eta(2)=L,\E_{r^{\prime},0}\eta(3)=D\}
:

(i)

Suppose
\eta(0)=\eta(1)=W
,
\eta(2)=L
and
\eta(3)=\eta(4)=D
. Then the event
\{\E_{r^{\prime},0}\eta(0)=L\}
happens with probability
1
, the event
\{\E_{r^{\prime},0}\eta(1)=W\}
happens with probability
(1-r^{\prime})
, the event
\{\E_{r^{\prime},0}\eta(2)=L\}
happens with probability
r^{\prime 2}
, and the event
\{\E_{r^{\prime},0}\eta(3)=D\}
happens with probability
(1-r^{\prime})(1+r^{\prime})
. Thus, the contribution of this case to
\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu(LWLD)
is
r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(WWLDD)
.

Therefore, letting
C_{LWLD}
denote the contribution from the cases not considered above, we obtain

(9.57)

\displaystyle\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu(LWLD)=r^{\prime 2}(1-r^{\prime})^{2}(1+r^{\prime})\mu(WWWLD)+C_{LWLD}.

Incorporating the new adjustment into (
9.56
), and emulating (

), we obtain:

\displaystyle w_{4}\left(\E_{r^{\prime},0}\mu\right)={}

9.4.
Detailed construction of the weight function for the bond percolation game when
(r^{\prime},s^{\prime})
belongs to the regime given by

We now demonstrate the step-by-step construction of our weight function for the regime given by

, i.e. for all

that satisfy the constraints

. The approach is the same the general idea outlined in §

). For

, we obtain, using reflection-invariance,

\displaystyle\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(D)={}

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle w_{1}(\mu)={}

\displaystyle w_{0}(\mu)-(2r^{\prime 2}-5r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(WDW)-(3r^{\prime}-10r^{\prime 2}+10r^{\prime 3}-4r^{\prime 4})\mu(WDL)

\displaystyle-(7r^{\prime 2}-7r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(DDW)-(2r^{\prime}-2r^{\prime 2}+5r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 4})\mu(DDL)

\displaystyle-(1-4r^{\prime}+6r^{\prime 2}-5r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(LDL)-(4r^{\prime 2}-r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(WLD)-(3r^{\prime 2}+3r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 4})\mu(LLD)

(9.64)

\displaystyle-(2r^{\prime}+8r^{\prime 2}-9r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(WWD),

which is exactly what we stated in (
9.9
).

In order to incorporate this adjustment into our current weight function inequality, following the idea used in the deduction of (
8.6
), we need to compute the pushforward probabilities
\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(WDW)
,
\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(WDL)
,
\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(DDW)
,
\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(DDL)
,
\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(LDL)
,
\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(WLD)
,
\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(LLD)
and
\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(WWD)
. However, we need only
partially
compute these expressions, since we only care about the contributions arising from cylinder sets that are able to negate, to as large an extent as possible, the term
-(-1+8r^{\prime}-5r^{\prime 2}+3r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 4})\mu(LWD)
. For instance,
\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(WDW)
is the probability of the event
\{\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\eta(0)=W,\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\eta(1)=D,\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\eta(2)=W\}
where
\eta
is a random configuration with law
\mu
, and we need only consider the contributions to this pushforward probability that arise from the event
\{\eta(1)=D,\eta(2)=W,\eta(3)=L\}
. In fact, this is precisely the event whose contributions we consider to each of the pushforward probabilities mentioned above, and we thereby obtain the following lower bounds:

\displaystyle\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(WDW)\geqslant{}

\displaystyle(1-r^{\prime}+r^{\prime 2})(1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})\{(2r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})\mu(WDWL)+(1-r^{\prime}+r^{\prime 2})\mu(LDWL)

\displaystyle+(2r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})\mu(DDWL)\}

\displaystyle\geqslant{}

\displaystyle(1-r^{\prime}+r^{\prime 2})(1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})(2r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})\mu(DWL)

(9.65)

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle\underbrace{(1-r^{\prime}+r^{\prime 2})(1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})(2r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})\mu(LWD)}_{\text{putting $\mu(DWL)=\mu(LWD)$, using reflection-invariance}};

\displaystyle\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(WDL)\geqslant

\displaystyle{}(1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})(r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})\{(2r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})\mu(WDWL)+(1-r^{\prime}+r^{\prime 2})\mu(LDWL)

(9.66)

\displaystyle+(2r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})\mu(DDWL)\}\geqslant(1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})(r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})(2r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})\mu(LWD);

\displaystyle\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(DDW)\geqslant{}

\displaystyle(1-r^{\prime}+r^{\prime 2})(1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})\{(1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})\mu(WDWL)+(r^{\prime}-2r^{\prime 2})\mu(LDWL)

(9.67)

\displaystyle+(1-2r^{\prime})\mu(DDWL)\}\geqslant(1-r^{\prime}+r^{\prime 2})(1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})(r^{\prime}-2r^{\prime 2})\mu(LWD);

\displaystyle\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(DDL)\geqslant{}

\displaystyle(r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})(1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})\{(1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})\mu(WDWL)+(r^{\prime}-2r^{\prime 2})\mu(LDWL)

(9.68)

\displaystyle+(1-2r^{\prime})\mu(DDWL)\}\geqslant(r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})(1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})(r^{\prime}-2r^{\prime 2})\mu(LWD);

\displaystyle\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(LDL)\geqslant{}

\displaystyle(r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})(1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})\{(r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})\mu(WDWL)+r^{\prime 2}\mu(LDWL)+r^{\prime 2}\mu(DDWL)\}

(9.69)

\displaystyle\geqslant{}

\displaystyle(r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})(1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})r^{\prime 2}\mu(LWD);

\displaystyle\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(WLD)\geqslant{}

\displaystyle(1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})(r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})\{(1-r^{\prime}+r^{\prime 2})\mu(WLWD)+(1-r^{\prime 2})\mu(LLWD)

(9.70)

\displaystyle+(1-r^{\prime}+r^{\prime 2})\mu(DLWD)\}\geqslant(1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})(r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})(1-r^{\prime}+r^{\prime 2})\mu(LWD);

\displaystyle\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(WWD)\geqslant{}

\displaystyle(1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})(1-r^{\prime}+r^{\prime 2})\{(1-r^{\prime}+r^{\prime 2})\mu(WLWD)+(1-r^{\prime 2})\mu(LLWD)

(9.71)

\displaystyle+(1-r^{\prime}+r^{\prime 2})\mu(DLWD)\}\geqslant(1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})(1-r^{\prime}+r^{\prime 2})^{2}\mu(LWD);

and finally,

\displaystyle\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(LLD)\geqslant{}

\displaystyle(1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})(r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})\{(r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})\mu(WLWD)+r^{\prime 2}\mu(LLWD)+r^{\prime 2}\mu(DLWD)\}

(9.72)

\displaystyle\geqslant{}

\displaystyle(1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})(r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})r^{\prime 2}\mu(LWD).

Incorporating the adjustment introduced in (
9.64
) into the weight function inequality in (
9.63
) via the idea demonstrated in (
8.6
), and using the lower bounds obtained in (

), (

), (

), (

), (

), (

), (

), we obtain

\displaystyle w_{1}(\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu)=

\displaystyle{}w_{1}(\mu)\underbrace{+(w_{0}(\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu)-w_{0}(\mu))}_{\text{substitute using \eqref{wt_fn_ineq_1_regime_2}}}-(2r^{\prime 2}-5r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(WDW)

\displaystyle-(3r^{\prime}-10r^{\prime 2}+10r^{\prime 3}-4r^{\prime 4})\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(WDL)-(7r^{\prime 2}-7r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(DDW)

\displaystyle-(2r^{\prime}-2r^{\prime 2}+5r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 4})\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(DDL)-(1-4r^{\prime}+6r^{\prime 2}-5r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(LDL)

\displaystyle-(4r^{\prime 2}-r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(WLD)-(3r^{\prime 2}+3r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 4})\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(LLD)

\displaystyle-(2r^{\prime}+8r^{\prime 2}-9r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(WWD)+(2r^{\prime 2}-5r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(WDW)

\displaystyle+(3r^{\prime}-10r^{\prime 2}+10r^{\prime 3}-4r^{\prime 4})\mu(WDL)+(7r^{\prime 2}-7r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(DDW)

\displaystyle+(2r^{\prime}-2r^{\prime 2}+5r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 4})\mu(DDL)+(1-4r^{\prime}+6r^{\prime 2}-5r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(LDL)

\displaystyle+(4r^{\prime 2}-r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(WLD)+(3r^{\prime 2}+3r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 4})\mu(LLD)

\displaystyle+(2r^{\prime}+8r^{\prime 2}-9r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(WWD)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{}w_{1}(\mu)\underbrace{-(2r^{\prime 2}-5r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(WDW)}_{\text{(i)}}\underbrace{-(3r^{\prime}-10r^{\prime 2}+10r^{\prime 3}-4r^{\prime 4})\mu(WDL)}_{\text{(ii)}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-(7r^{\prime 2}-7r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(DDW)}_{\text{(iii)}}\underbrace{-(2r^{\prime}-2r^{\prime 2}+5r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 4})\mu(DDL)}_{\text{(iv)}}

\displaystyle-(5r^{\prime 2}-2r^{\prime 3})\mu(DDD)\underbrace{-(1-4r^{\prime}+6r^{\prime 2}-5r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(LDL)}_{\text{(v)}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-(4r^{\prime 2}-r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(WLD)}_{\text{(vi)}}\underbrace{-(3r^{\prime 2}+3r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 4})\mu(LLD)}_{\text{(vii)}}

\displaystyle-(4r^{\prime 2}-r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(DLD)\underbrace{-(2r^{\prime}+8r^{\prime 2}-9r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(WWD)}_{\text{(viii)}}

\displaystyle-(-1+8r^{\prime}-5r^{\prime 2}+3r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 4})\mu(LWD)-(2r^{\prime}+8r^{\prime 2}-9r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(DWD)

\displaystyle-(2r^{\prime 2}-5r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(WDW)-(3r^{\prime}-10r^{\prime 2}+10r^{\prime 3}-4r^{\prime 4})\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(WDL)

\displaystyle-(7r^{\prime 2}-7r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(DDW)-(2r^{\prime}-2r^{\prime 2}+5r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 4})\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(DDL)

\displaystyle-(1-4r^{\prime}+6r^{\prime 2}-5r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(LDL)-(4r^{\prime 2}-r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(WLD)

\displaystyle-(3r^{\prime 2}+3r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 4})\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(LLD)-(2r^{\prime}+8r^{\prime 2}-9r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(WWD)

\displaystyle\underbrace{+(2r^{\prime 2}-5r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(WDW)}_{\text{(i)}}\underbrace{+(3r^{\prime}-10r^{\prime 2}+10r^{\prime 3}-4r^{\prime 4})\mu(WDL)}_{\text{(ii)}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{+(7r^{\prime 2}-7r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(DDW)}_{\text{(iii)}}\underbrace{+(2r^{\prime}-2r^{\prime 2}+5r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 4})\mu(DDL)}_{\text{(iv)}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{+(1-4r^{\prime}+6r^{\prime 2}-5r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(LDL)}_{\text{(v)}}\underbrace{+(4r^{\prime 2}-r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(WLD)}_{\text{(vi)}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{+(3r^{\prime 2}+3r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 4})\mu(LLD)}_{\text{(vii)}}\underbrace{+(2r^{\prime}+8r^{\prime 2}-9r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(WWD)}_{\text{(viii)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{}w_{1}(\mu)-(5r^{\prime 2}-2r^{\prime 3})\mu(DDD)-(4r^{\prime 2}-r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(DLD)

\displaystyle-(-1+8r^{\prime}-5r^{\prime 2}+3r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 4})\mu(LWD)-(2r^{\prime}+8r^{\prime 2}-9r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(DWD)

\displaystyle\underbrace{-(2r^{\prime 2}-5r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(WDW)}_{\text{use \eqref{Fpp_WDW}}}\underbrace{-(3r^{\prime}-10r^{\prime 2}+10r^{\prime 3}-4r^{\prime 4})\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(WDL)}_{\text{use \eqref{Fpp_WDL}}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-(7r^{\prime 2}-7r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(DDW)}_{\text{use \eqref{Fpp_DDW}}}\underbrace{-(2r^{\prime}-2r^{\prime 2}+5r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 4})\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(DDL)}_{\text{use \eqref{Fpp_DDL}}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-(1-4r^{\prime}+6r^{\prime 2}-5r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(LDL)}_{\text{use \eqref{Fpp_LDL}}}\underbrace{-(4r^{\prime 2}-r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(WLD)}_{\text{use \eqref{Fpp_WLD}}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-(3r^{\prime 2}+3r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 4})\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(LLD)}_{\text{use \eqref{Fpp_LLD}}}\underbrace{-(2r^{\prime}+8r^{\prime 2}-9r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\E_{r^{\prime},r^{\prime}}\mu(WWD)}_{\text{use \eqref{Fpp_WWD}}}

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle{}w_{1}(\mu)-(5r^{\prime 2}-2r^{\prime 3})\mu(DDD)-(4r^{\prime 2}-r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(DLD)

\displaystyle\underbrace{-(-1+8r^{\prime}-5r^{\prime 2}+3r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 4})\mu(LWD)}-(2r^{\prime}+8r^{\prime 2}-9r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(DWD)

\displaystyle\underbrace{-(2r^{\prime 2}-5r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})((1-r^{\prime}+r^{\prime 2})(1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})(2r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})\mu(LWD))}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-(3r^{\prime}-10r^{\prime 2}+10r^{\prime 3}-4r^{\prime 4})((1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})(r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})(2r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})\mu(LWD))}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-(7r^{\prime 2}-7r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})((1-r^{\prime}+r^{\prime 2})(1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})(r^{\prime}-2r^{\prime 2})\mu(LWD))}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-(2r^{\prime}-2r^{\prime 2}+5r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 4})((r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})(1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})(r^{\prime}-2r^{\prime 2})\mu(LWD))}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-(1-4r^{\prime}+6r^{\prime 2}-5r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})((r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})(1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})r^{\prime 2}\mu(LWD))}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-(4r^{\prime 2}-r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})((1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})(r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})(1-r^{\prime}+r^{\prime 2})\mu(LWD))}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-(3r^{\prime 2}+3r^{\prime 3}-2r^{\prime 4})((1-r^{\prime})(1-2r^{\prime})(r^{\prime}-r^{\prime 2})r^{\prime 2}\mu(LWD))}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{}w_{1}(\mu)-(5r^{\prime 2}-2r^{\prime 3})\mu(DDD)-(4r^{\prime 2}-r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(DLD)-(2r^{\prime}+8r^{\prime 2}-9r^{\prime 3}+2r^{\prime 4})\mu(DWD)

\displaystyle\underbrace{+(1-10r^{\prime}+7r^{\prime 2}+64r^{\prime 4}-292r^{\prime 5}+583r^{\prime 6}-663r^{\prime 7}+447r^{\prime 8}-168r^{\prime 9}+28r^{\prime 10})\mu(LWD)}_{\text{obtained by combining the terms highlighted above}}

\displaystyle\leqslant

\displaystyle{}w_{1}(\mu)+(1-10r^{\prime}+7r^{\prime 2}+64r^{\prime 4}-292r^{\prime 5}+583r^{\prime 6}-663r^{\prime 7}+447r^{\prime 8}-168r^{\prime 9}+28r^{\prime 10})\mu(LWD),

which is exactly what we stated in (

). This brings us to the conclusion of the construction of our weight function for

belonging to (
